#### 205. RPV-27: Negative marker anchor is the vertex mean; Windows uses the area-weighted path centroid
- owner: rendering-planview | impact: ui-cosmetic | disposition: fix | testable: needs-extraction-from-objc | verdict: CONFIRMED | status: fixed
- Windows: `takeoff_renderer.py:390-437, 464-483`: Marker centre = polygon area centroid of the item path (MoveTo/LineTo points), falling back to the nearest interior anchor when the centroid is outside the shape, or the vertex mean for zero area.
- iOS (as mapped): `PlanView/PlanView.mm:346-355`: Arithmetic mean of the transformed vertices (for count shapes, the position point).
- Windows tests: tests/presentation/visualization/pdf/renderers/test_takeoff_renderer.py::test_area_with_hole_uses_anchor_inside_visible_fill; ::test_the_marker_is_drawn_over_a_negative_takeoff
- note (Phase 1): Linear and symmetric count shapes agree.
- outcome (fixed): The negative marker sits at Windows' _path_centroid of the takeoff path: Core negative_marker_anchor / canvas_marker_anchor = area-weighted centroid of the path points (closeSubpath repeats the start point, so a zero-area path gives the mean WITH that point), else, when Qt's QPainterPath.contains says the centroid is outside, the nearest interior point (vertex fractions then... Area with backouts: Windows uses the point order of Qt QPathClipper (not reproducible); iOS uses the region centroid with the Windows contract (anchor inside the visible fill). DECIDED 2026-11-19 (keep, no code change): (a) the area-weighted-centroid anchor of the region with a nearest-interior fallback and (b) the marker at the centroid of the displayed shape on rotated or flipped pages both stay. Possible Windows defect for (b): takeoff_renderer.py:105-116 and :390-417 + zoom_handler.py:339-348, :362-379 draw the marker displaced from its shape on a rotated or flipped page. Files: `PlanView/PlanView.mm, PlanView/PlanView.h, PlanView/PlanView_Internal.h, Core/Geometry/takeoff_footprint.hpp, Core/Geometry/takeoff_footprint.cpp`.
- decision (2026-11-19): keep iOS: (a) The negative marker anchor (area-weighted centroid of the region with a nearest-interior fallback) stays; (b) the marker position at the centroid of the displayed shape on rotated or flipped pages stays. No code change. Pinned by: MarkerAnchor.MatchesPathCentroidOfTheWindowsRenderer; MarkerAnchor.AnchorOfAnAreaWithAHoleIsInsideTheVisibleFill; MarkerAnchor.CountTriangleAndCurvedBand; MarkerAnchor.PolygonAreaCentroid. CI note (2026-11-20 UTC): on arm64 (AppleClang 17) MarkerAnchor.CountTriangleAndCurvedBand failed once (x 77.4244 against 72.5756): on a mirror-symmetric curved band the two best grid centres of the marker-anchor search are mirror images whose squared distances tie bit for bit on the x86-64 builds (MSVC, clang), so the first wins, as in Windows; the arm64 run picked the mirror one. The cause, a last-bit libm difference in the atan2/cos/sin arc points, is inferred and was not observed on arm64. The assertion now accepts the Windows x or its mirror (150 - x), and a libm-free test, MarkerAnchor.AnExactTieKeepsTheFirstCandidateOfTheWindowsOrder, pins the tie rule; no app code changed. On a device the marker of an exactly mirror-symmetric curved band may sit one grid cell (4.85 points at the test scale) on the other side of its axis than on Windows (see PORTING_BLOCKERS.md, Not reproducible on iOS).
- possible Windows defect ((b) marker on rotated or flipped pages): ost_visualizer/presentation/visualization/pdf/renderers/takeoff_renderer.py:105-116; ost_visualizer/presentation/visualization/pdf/renderers/takeoff_renderer.py:390-417; ost_visualizer/presentation/components/plan_view/components/zoom_handler.py:339-348; ost_visualizer/presentation/components/plan_view/components/zoom_handler.py:362-379: The negative marker is created with setPos(centroid) and ItemIgnoresTransformations, and _apply_page_transform_to_items then calls setTransform(page transform) on every takeoff item including the markers. The page transform contains a translation about the page centre, and for an item that ignores view transformations the item's own transform is applied in device space, so on a rotated or flipped page the marker is drawn displaced from its shape, by an amount that also changes with the zoom. Scenario: Rotate a page by 90 degrees (or flip it) in a bid that has a negative takeoff: the red minus marker is drawn away from the takeoff it marks. Severity: cosmetic, confidence: medium. Suggested Windows fix: Do not apply the page transform to the marker items (skip items with ItemIgnoresTransformations in _apply_page_transform_to_items) and instead position them by mapping the centroid through the transform, for example setPos(transform.map(centroid)).

#### 206. RPV-29: 3D lighting constants and two-sided normal flip differ
- owner: rendering-planview | impact: ui-cosmetic | disposition: wont-fix (Phase 1: fix; was needs-decision until 2026-11-19) | testable: objc-only | verdict: CONFIRMED | status: wont-fix

#### 214. LP-24: Layer list ordering ties and merged template layers
- owner: layers-pages | impact: ui-cosmetic | disposition: wont-fix (Phase 1: fix; was needs-decision until 2026-11-19) | testable: needs-extraction-from-objc | verdict: AMBIGUOUS | status: wont-fix
- Windows: `ost_visualizer/domain/entities/layer.py:89-93; infrastructure/mdb/components/bid_data_reader.py:575-615`: The sidebar lists bid layers plus locked template layers (IsTemplate and IsLocked), merged by lowercased name with the bid row winning, sorted by Sequence only (stable; ties keep template-first then database order).
- iOS (as mapped): `ViewController+Layers.mm:12-24; ProjectStore+Load.cpp:103-121`: Lists only WHERE BidUID rows and sorts by (sequence, name). Locked template layers never appear, and ties sort alphabetically.
- Windows tests: tests/domain/entities/test_layer.py::MergeLayersForBidTests; tests/domain/entities/test_layer.py::LayerEntityContractTests::test_merge_prefers_the_later_sequence_for_equal_non_template_names
- note (Phase 1): No ORDER BY in the Windows layer query, so tie behavior is effectively undefined. Verifier: Windows lists bid layers plus locked template layers merged by lowercased name and sorted by Sequence only; ...
- decision 2026-11-16: option (a) Port the merge_layers_for_bid rule (template merge, sequence sort, stable ties). User: "port merge_layers_for_bid"
- fix scope (set by the decision, owner layers-pages): Port merge_layers_for_bid (template merge, sequence sort, stable ties).
- outcome (wont-fix): Merge, Sequence sort and stable ties are done (merge_layers_for_bid, ViewController+Layers.mm). Show All / Hide All change only the bid own layers; a template-only entry cannot be toggled. DECIDED 2026-11-19 (keep iOS, no code change). Windows layer_operations.py:259-300 update_all_layers_show also writes the Show of locked template layer rows (shared by every bid) while update_layer_show (:43-55) refuses them: possible Windows defect (inconsistent). Pinned by Dbp35_BulkVisibilitySelection.CommentsTemplateOnlyAndMergedAwayLayersAreLeftAlone. Files: `Core/Domain/Layer.hpp, Core/Data/ProjectData.hpp, Core/Persistence/ProjectStore/Components/ProjectStore+Load.cpp, Controllers/ViewController/Components/ViewController+Layers.mm`.
- decision (2026-11-19): keep iOS: Show All / Hide All change only the bid's own layers; the locked template layers shared with other bids are never written and a template-only entry cannot be toggled. No code change. Pinned by: Dbp35_BulkVisibilitySelection.CommentsTemplateOnlyAndMergedAwayLayersAreLeftAlone; Dbp35_BulkVisibilitySelection.OnlyTheLayersThatDifferAreChanged.
- possible Windows defect: ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/layer_operations.py:259-300; ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/layer_operations.py:43-55; ost_visualizer/infrastructure/database/bid_owned_identity.py:205-235; ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/bid_data_reader.py:581-615; ost_visualizer/infrastructure/mdb/components/bid_operations.py:595-637; ost_visualizer/presentation/components/layers_sidebar.py:235-238; ost_visualizer/application/services/project_write_service.py:6112-6142; tests/infrastructure/mdb/components/test_layer_operations.py:335; tests/infrastructure/mdb/components/test_layer_operations.py:365; tests/infrastructure/mdb/components/test_layer_operations.py:630: The same shared template row can be hidden with Hide All (written, and visible to every bid that has no own copy) but not with its own checkbox (IncoherentBidOwnedScopeError, the toggle reverts). Both behaviours are pinned by Windows tests with intent-bearing names, so this is a deliberate but contradictory design, not an obvious bug. Exposure is narrow: create_bid copies the templates as bid rows and the merge lets the bid row win, so only bids without their own copy are affected. Scenario: Hide All in one bid hides the shared template layer in another bid that has no own copy, while the layer's own checkbox cannot be toggled at all. Severity: inconsistent, confidence: low. Suggested Windows fix: Decide one rule for shared template rows: either let the single toggle persist them through the template path (update_default_layer_show) or drop the template rows from the bulk write and the captured uids.

#### 215. MSH-21: Flat quantized face shading instead of Windows' 60-degree crease-angle smooth shading
- owner: rendering-planview | impact: ui-cosmetic | disposition: wont-fix | testable: core-cpp | verdict: CONFIRMED | status: wont-fix

- note (Phase 1): The iOS log (2026-03-23, 'Quantized flat shading') records this as a deliberate rendering choice to avoid seam artifacts, so it is a decision, not a straight port. Rendering is a platform layer.
- decision 2026-11-16: option (b) Keep flat shading (rendering-layer choice, no numbers affected). User: "keep flat shading (rendering-layer choice, no numbers affected)"
- closed (wont-fix): Won't fix. User decision 2026-11-16, option (b): "keep flat shading (rendering-layer choice, no numbers affected)". Item reason: Flat quantized shading is a deliberate rendering-layer choice (PORTING_LOG 2026-03-23, avoids seam artifacts); no numbers are affected.
- outcome (wont-fix): Closed by user decision: Won't fix. User decision 2026-11-16, option (b): "keep flat shading (rendering-layer choice, no numbers affected)". Item reason: Flat quantized shading is a deliberate rendering-layer choice (PORTING_LOG 2026-03-23, avoids seam artifacts); no numbers are affected. Files: `Rendering/MeshLoader.cpp:26-66 (flat face normals snapped to 1/2048; smooth vertex normals computed but unused); Renderer.mm:376-395; Rendering/Shaders/src/fs_mesh.sc`.

#### 216. EXP-24: DXF LINE entity order is the std::unordered_set iteration order of the STL in use
- owner: export-formats | impact: ui-cosmetic | disposition: cannot-reproduce | testable: core-cpp | verdict: CANNOT_REPRODUCE | status: cannot-reproduce
- Windows: `cpp_extensions/src/mesh/feature_edges.cpp:109-175; exporters/dxf_exporter.py:32-38`: Edges come from an unordered_set in MSVC STL iteration order, so the LINE order in the DXF is MSVC-specific.
- iOS (as mapped): `Core/Geometry/feature_edges.cpp:109-175; Services/ExportService.mm:153-155`: The same algorithm on libc++, whose unordered_set iteration order differs. LINE order (and which endpoint is first in a LINE) can differ from Windows.
- Windows tests: none found
- note (Phase 1): CAD semantics are order-independent. The DXFWriter itself is byte-identical for the same line list. Verifier: LINE entity order follows unordered_set iteration (feature_edges.cpp:109-175), which is MSVC STL specific and cannot be matched under libc++. ...
- outcome (cannot-reproduce): Not reproducible on iOS: DXF entity order is the iteration order of std::unordered_set of the STL in use (open disposition, nothing to port). Files: `Core/Geometry/feature_edges.cpp:109-173; Services/ExportService.mm:151`.

## (E) Decisions (44 of 44 decided 2026-11-16; 23 items decided 2026-11-19) and what is still open

All 44 items that were `needs-decision` in the Phase 1 map were answered by the user on 2026-11-16 (44 of 44 decisions recorded): 31 were set to `fix` (the user's words define the scope) and 13 were closed as `wont-fix` (GEO-15 and EXP-17 only for winding and vertex-order parity; the triangulation sets stay with GEO-14). MSH-16, EXP-22 and MSH-19b did not pick a map option: the user chose to follow the FIXED Windows behaviour found by the re-baseline (now committed there as `6d4786f`). Nothing from the map is `needs-decision` any more. **Applied (final, 2026-11-19):** 24 fixed, 5 fixed-ui-unverified, 0 blocked-needs-decision, 15 wont-fix (on 2026-11-18 it was 25, 4, 2 and 13). The two decisions that were `blocked-needs-decision` on 2026-11-18 (IMP-20, LP-24) are applied for the decided part; their open sub-points were decided on 2026-11-19 (keep iOS), so both items are `wont-fix` now (see [Decisions of 2026-11-19](#decisions-of-2026-11-19)). The 5 `fixed-ui-unverified` decisions are POS-M01, DBP-31, BRW-08, BRW-16 and COND-STYLE-02 (moved from fixed on 2026-11-19): ported, UI part needs a device check. Full evidence is in the item blocks (sections A-D) and `PARITY_DIVERGENCES.json` (repo root); the decision itself is stored in each record under `decision`, with `queueNote` or `closedReason`.

**Standing rule (user, 2026-11-16).** If a Windows behavior looks accidental, internally inconsistent or unsafe, do NOT port it: it goes into the report's 'needs my decision' list with the Windows file:line and what was done meanwhile (keep the current iOS behavior). Since 2026-11-19 that list is the [Still open](#still-open) section below; the 8 formal items and the 15 candidate items that were on it are decided (see [Decisions of 2026-11-19](#decisions-of-2026-11-19)).

### Decisions that follow the re-baselined Windows behavior

- **MSH-16** (fixed, export-formats): one rule for every export format and the 3D view: a negative subtracts only from positives of the same condition type, a fully consumed positive is dropped, negatives are never output. One shared Core boolean pass (`Core/Meshing/BooleanPass.*`) called by ExportService.mm and ProjectService.mm; delivered together with RB-01, RB-02, RB-03, EXP-05 and MSH-15 (all fixed).
- **EXP-22** (fixed, export-formats): a negative-only export (or any export where nothing survives the boolean pass) reports "Nothing to Export" in every format (OBJ, DXF, HTML) and writes no file (RB-02); a fully consumed positive is dropped. The wording of the alert was a candidate question (EXP-21); the user kept the iOS wording on 2026-11-19.
- **MSH-19b** (fixed, meshing): iOS already kept the plain wall when the wall and drop-stub union fails; the warning log with the takeoff UID, the empty-face union counted as failure and the catch of a throw (shared with RB-04) are done in `Core/Meshing/MeshFactory.cpp`.

### Cross-references created by the decisions

- **GEO-15** and **EXP-17** (wont-fix for winding / byte-order parity): triangulation sets are fixed by GEO-14 (status fixed; equivalence tests, no ordering/winding change). iOS meshes stay outward-facing.
- **RPV-22** (wont-fix for curved placement and the centre handle): its two remaining obligations are carried by GEO-18 (draw existing curves) and RPV-M03 (a drag must not corrupt the control point); both are fixed.
- **POS-M01**, **DBP-31** and **BRW-16** share one outcome: the bid open fails with an error naming the cause (takeoff UID, missing reference, cycle); no silent drops (all three fixed-ui-unverified: the alert is UIKit).
- **IMP-17** (wont-fix): stored + MSZIP stay; unsupported compression types fail with a clear error (done together with IMP-15 and IMP-M03 in Core/Import/CabExtractor.cpp).

### Decisions of 2026-11-19

On 2026-11-19 the user closed the open blockers (message "Close out the open porting blockers"). The 8 formal items that were `blocked-needs-decision` and the 15 candidate items that sat inside otherwise fixed items were decided: **21 keep-iOS decisions** without a code change (19 items; IMP-20 has three sub-rules, RPV-27 (a) and (b) count as one) and **4 code changes**, each written test-first (COND-STYLE-02 and COND-ELEV-01 deliberately deviate from the literal Windows behaviour, DLG-FINITE-01 only for its Rise / Run half, UND-09 ports Windows). Status rule: the 8 formal items DBP-04 (covers DBP-05), BRW-09, EXP-12, EXP-13, EXP-16, IMP-20, LP-24 and RPV-29 changed from `blocked-needs-decision` to `wont-fix` (kept by user decision 2026-11-19; for IMP-20 the decided DATETIME rule itself is fixed and tested); the candidate items keep their status and the question inside them is marked decided, except COND-STYLE-02 and COND-ELEV-01, which are `fixed-ui-unverified` now because their Obj-C part was then only syntax-checked (it has since also compiled against the real iOS SDK on CI, 2026-11-20 UTC; it has not run on a device or simulator). Everything the message did not mention stays as it was. Machine-readable: `header.decisions2026_11_19` and the per-item `decisionUpdate` in `PARITY_DIVERGENCES.json`.

DBP-33: the decision text of 2026-11-16 is corrected (NameFontColor is a Windows default, so the explicit list is Count, GridOffsetX/Y, GridRotation, FontColor and NameFontColor; the text of IsNegativeQuantity was checked against the code and the record is consistent, it needs no entry because insert_takeoff writes it on every insert as the takeoff's own flag). The decision was not widened.

#### Code changes of 2026-11-19 (all with host tests written first)

- **COND-STYLE-02** (code change, deliberate deviation from the literal Windows behavior; status fixed-ui-unverified): On a style change in a metric bid, the metric type defaults are persisted: style_dimension_defaults(type, metric) (spacing 100 mm / Area 150 mm; thickness 100 / 100 / 100 / 0 mm for Linear / Area / Count / Attachment), not the imperial table Windows writes (edit_condition_dialog.py:1445-1448). Deliberate deviation from the literal Windows behavior; the imperial behavior is unchanged and equals Windows. The spacing rule of the 2026-11-16 decision (a) is unchanged. Files: Core/Domain/ConditionStyleChange.hpp; Core/Domain/ConditionStyleChange.cpp; Controllers/EditConditionViewController/EditConditionViewController.mm (one line: passes _metric); Config/Tests/Conditions/test_style_change.cpp; Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_form.cpp (explicit false on 3 calls). Tests added: CondStyle02_Spacing.AMetricBidPersistsTheMetricTypeDefaultsForEveryStyleChange; CondStyle02_Spacing.AnImperialBidKeepsTheWindowsTableForEveryStyleChange; CondStyle02_Spacing.AMetricBidKeepsWhatTheUserTypedAfterAStyleChange; CondStyle02_Spacing.AMetricSaveWithoutAStyleChangeWritesNoDefaults; CondM08_UnsupportedType.ASaveRewritesTheTypeWithTheMetricDefaultsInAMetricBid; CondStyle02_Spacing.AMetricBidWritesTheMetricThicknessWhenTheFieldEqualsTheStoredOne (added by the reviewer). Tests changed: CondStyle02_Spacing.TheTypeChangeDefaultsAreTheWindowsTableValues -> renamed TheTypeChangeDefaultsFollowTheMeasurementSystemOfTheBid (the literal-Windows block for a metric bid replaced by the metric decision; imperial assertions kept). Failing first: With the explicit metric parameter added and the old behavior kept, the Conditions host tests gave 163 tests, 159 passed, 4 failed (assertions such as CHECK_EQ(type_change_defaults(Linear, true).spacing, 3.937007874015748) failed: 4 != 3.937007874015748); the imperial matrix test passed as a regression guard. Review: DEFECT-FIXED-BY-REVIEWER. Production code correct. One test gap (metric thickness in the field-equals-stored branch survived a mutation) was closed by the reviewer with AMetricBidWritesTheMetricThicknessWhenTheFieldEqualsTheStoredOne; mutations M1, M3, M4 are killed, M2 was killed after the new test. The .mm argument _metric is not host-mutable. Not verified: The Edit Condition screen in a metric bid was not seen on a device (fixed-ui-unverified). After a style change the Spacing field keeps its old text while Save writes the new style's type default unless the field was edited (decision (a), as on Windows).
- **COND-ELEV-01** (code change, deliberate deviation from the literal Windows behavior; status fixed-ui-unverified): After a Save, is_top and z_value are derived from the condition name with extract_z_value_from_name (ost_domain::derive_elevation_from_name; an unreadable name gives 0.0 / false), so the in-memory condition equals what a reload gives. Deliberate deviation from the dialog's own values; it matches the effective Windows behavior, which reloads the conditions from the database after every save. Files: Core/Domain/PositionParser.hpp; Core/Domain/PositionParser.cpp; Core/Domain/ConditionStyleChange.cpp; Core/Domain/ConditionStyleChange.hpp; Core/Domain/ConditionFormRules.hpp (comment); Core/Persistence/ProjectStore/ProjectStore.cpp (extract_z_value now wraps the shared function); Controllers/EditConditionViewController/EditConditionViewController.mm; Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_elevation.cpp (new); Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_form.cpp (comment only). Tests added: CondElev01_SaveDerivesFromName.* (5); CondElev01_SharedDerivation.* (2); CondElev01_ReloadParity.TheSavedInMemoryConditionEqualsTheReloadedOne. Failing first: Six of the seven new tests failed on assertions before the change (for example the oracle-row check counted 3559 mismatches instead of 0; the round trip through the real store: 244 of 261 conditions differed between memory and reload). The seventh calls the new function and could not exist before. (The test file has 8 new tests now, 5 + 2 + 1; the first-run record names seven.) Review: CONFIRMED-FIXED. No defect found, no edits. Windows evidence re-read, including that Windows reloads after a save (project_write_service.py:1284-1340, :1160-1205). Not verified: The dialog / save / 3D redraw path was not run on a device (fixed-ui-unverified). Elevation text the loader does not parse reloads as (0.0, false), for example a metric "3200" without a unit and decimal inches such as "10' 6.3\"".
- **DLG-FINITE-01** (code change, port the Windows behavior; status fixed): Whitespace-only Rise and Run are rejected with 'Rise must be a number.' / 'Run must be a number.' like Windows (only an EMPTY field is 0). ONLY this half is decided: the condition-number half (iOS refuses 2147483648 and up, Windows accepts up to the 64-bit column) is NOT decided and stays open. Files: Core/Domain/ConditionFormRules.cpp; Core/Domain/ConditionFormRules.hpp (comment); Controllers/EditConditionViewController/EditConditionViewController.mm (comment only); Config/Tests/Conditions/test_condition_form.cpp. Tests added: DlgFinite01_RiseRun.AWhiteSpaceOnlyFieldIsNotANumberLikeWindows; DlgFinite01_RiseRun.WhiteSpaceAroundANumberAndTheOtherRefusalsStayAsTheyWere. Tests changed: CondVal01_RiseRun.ABlankFieldIsZeroAndANumberIsTheNumber -> renamed AnEmptyFieldIsZeroAndANumberIsTheNumber (the "   " case that pinned the old behavior is replaced by the empty-field case). Failing first: The new whitespace test failed on 15 texts x Rise/Run x Linear/Area before the change (CHECK(issue.has_value()) failed; value 0 != -1); a filtered run gave 5 tests, 4 passed and 1 failed (DlgFinite01_RiseRun.AWhiteSpaceOnlyFieldIsNotANumberLikeWindows). Review: CONFIRMED-FIXED by an independent reviewer. Mutation: putting back the old strip in parse_slope_value makes the new whitespace test fail on 120 assertions (60 rows) while the empty-field, C0-separator and neighbouring-behavior tests keep passing; the file was restored byte-identically (sha256). Other plausible wrong fixes are caught as well (rejecting empty text, the finite-number wording, stripping around a number). Limit: that UITextField.text returns exactly what was typed or pasted is an assumption; Rise and Run use a decimal pad with no space key, so a whitespace-only value arrives only by paste or an external keyboard. Checked as well: the diff against the version before the change (the Core change in parse_slope_value plus comments); the .mm hands the raw field text to Core (Rise and Run are not in the focus-out normalisation set OSTIsDimensionTag).
- **UND-09** (code change, port the Windows behavior; status fixed-ui-unverified): 'Count as Negative Quantity' changes ONLY the pressed takeoff, in ONE undoable entry (like Windows on_set_negative for a single selected takeoff). A condition that is merely selected in the sidebar no longer widens the change to every takeoff of the condition on the open pages. Files: Core/Domain/NegativeChange.hpp (new); Controllers/ViewController/ViewController.mm; Controllers/ViewController/Components/UndoRedoController.h; Controllers/ViewController/Components/UndoRedoController.mm; Config/Tests/Settings/test_negative_press.cpp (new). Tests added: Und09_NegativePress.* (6 tests). Failing first: the Core press decision was first added with the old rule (a condition selected in the sidebar widens the change to every takeoff of the condition on the open pages); against it 5 of the 6 new tests failed on assertions (for example changes.size 3 != 1, and in the undo chain test takeoff 500 was changed too) and 1 passed (AnUnknownUidGivesNoChange, which the old rule already met): TESTS: 6 PASSED: 1 FAILED: 5. The red run was against that old rule placed in Core, not against the .mm (the host harness cannot run .mm); negative_values was moved verbatim from the .mm. Review: CONFIRMED-FIXED by an independent reviewer, who ran three mutations of NegativeChange.hpp (the file was restored byte-identically, checked by sha256): widening the press to the whole condition (the old behavior) failed 5 of 6 tests; recording no old flag and returning a change for an unknown uid failed 5 of 6; swapping use_old in negative_values failed 2 of 6 (undo and redo swapped). The .mm call site cannot be mutated on the host, so the reviewer read it. The Core test has no selection input: it proves one change per press with the old flag recorded, not that the .mm passes the right uid and flag. The reviewer also diffed the three .mm/.h files against the version before the change (the selection logic moved into ost_domain::negative_changes_for_press, one history entry, undo and redo through negative_values). The tests exercise Core only; _setNegative:forPressedTakeoff:, the context menu and the undo buttons are only syntax-checked against stubs. Not verified: The context-menu / undo / redo path was not run on a device (fixed-ui-unverified).

Tests added by the four changes: 22 (Conditions +16: COND-STYLE-02 +6 including one added by the reviewer, COND-ELEV-01 +8, DLG-FINITE-01 +2; Settings +6: UND-09). The Obj-C parts of all four were syntax-checked against hand-written stubs (they have since compiled against the real iPhoneOS 18.5 SDK on CI, 2026-11-20 UTC) and were not run on a device or simulator.

#### Kept, researched, not a defect

Kept iOS behaviours whose Windows side was researched and judged NOT a Windows defect (28 sub-items): deliberate, test-pinned or harmless design choices.

- **DBP-04** (decided 2026-11-19): NULL / missing TakeoffIncrements loads as 1.0 (already ported): Not a defect.
- **BRW-09** (decided 2026-11-19): restore from Deleted Bids with a vanished OrigBidProjectUID fails the whole restore group: Not a defect: a deliberate referential-integrity rule.
- **DBP-33** (decided 2026-11-19): NameFontColor default (iOS writes 0 on insert): Not a defect.
- **EXP-21** (decided 2026-11-19): export error text 'Export function returned False': Not a defect: deliberate and test-pinned wording.
- **EXP-09** (decided 2026-11-19): page order = click order in multi-page exports: Not a defect: a harmless design choice.
- **IMP-20** (decided 2026-11-19): (2) BidTexts.Name and BidCallOuts.Name encoded as latin-1 bytes, raising for characters outside latin-1 (iOS stores UTF-8 text): Not a defect (on the import side): the legacy column is a latin-1 byte blob and Windows deliberately raises instead of silently replacing characters; decoding latin-1 never fails, so an OST/OSP produced by Windows always re-encodes.
- **IMP-20** (decided 2026-11-19): (3) memo and IMAGE (longbinary) text stored as a UTF-8 BLOB (iOS keeps TEXT): Not a defect: deliberate and consistent.
- **COND-FOLDER-01** (decided 2026-11-19): cdn_type_name sentinel 'Unknown' in the loader (iOS keeps ''): Not a defect: 'Unknown' for no type and for a dangling type uid is a deliberate loader convention, mirrored by the entity default and several consumers; the dialog hides it (uid lookup first, so a real type named 'Unknown' still works).
- **COND-ELEV-01** (decided 2026-11-19): is_top / z_value taken from the dialog but memory comes from a reload: Nothing of consequence: the two DTO keys are never persisted and Windows reloads the conditions from the database after every save, so Windows memory always equals what a reload derives from the name.
- **RPV-29** (decided 2026-11-19): Normal flip in the 3D lighting (iOS flips towards the viewer, Windows orients normals to +z): Not a defect on its own: orienting shading normals to +z is deliberate and test-pinned, and the shader lights with the raw normal on purpose.
- **RPV-26** (decided 2026-11-19): Minimum interactive sizes (8 px count item / 2 px linear band at Windows view_scale 3.0; iOS uses 8/3 pt and 2/3 pt): Not a Windows defect: the minimums are fixed pixel constants in scene pixels and are consistent in the renderer and the drag/placement code.
- **RPV-27** (decided 2026-11-19): (a) negative marker anchor of an area with backouts (Qt subtracted-path point order versus iOS region centroid): Not a defect: Windows feeds the points of the Qt-subtracted path (several subpaths concatenated) into one polygon centroid, which is not the area centroid of the visible fill, but the contract (anchor inside the visible fill, with an interior fallback) is test-pinned and met.
- **RPV-16** (decided 2026-11-19): Page switch also clears the selected / place condition and the FAB placement availability on iOS (Windows keeps the sidebar condition highlighted): Not a defect: Windows drops the takeoff selection on a page switch (pinned) and leaves the sidebar condition highlighted; iOS clearing the condition as well is the safe direction.
- **UND-02** (decided 2026-11-19): insert_takeoffs validates every parent against rows that exist BEFORE the batch (iOS accepts a parent restored earlier in the same batch): Not a Windows defect: Windows never restores a takeoff under its original uid (the writer allocates new uids), so a parent has to exist before the insert.
- **GEO-M02** (decided 2026-11-19): drag validity (polygon_is_valid) evaluated on scene coordinates instead of OST coordinates: Not a practical defect: validity is decided on coordinates scaled by OST * 72 * view_scale / scale_ratio, so for exactly collinear or touching (degenerate) polygons the answer can depend on floating-point rounding of that scale factor.
- **EXP-15** (decided 2026-11-16): HTML export includes hidden-layer conditions (iOS filters them): Not a defect: deliberate and test-pinned.
- **EXP-18** (decided 2026-11-16): export page and takeoff order follows the user's selection order (iOS sorts): Not a defect: deliberate and pinned.
- **IMP-17** (decided 2026-11-16): LZX and Quantum cabinet compression (iOS decodes stored and MSZIP only): Not a Windows problem.
- **DBP-34** (decided 2026-11-16): boolean columns: Windows writes the Access Yes/No true value (-1), iOS mixes 1 and -1: Not a Windows defect.
- **DBP-M05** (decided 2026-11-16): per-page selected area (BidPageSettings.BidAreaSelected) and inactive-area colouring: Not a defect: a deliberate Windows feature (areas are surfaced there, not on iOS).
- **COND-EXTRA-01** (decided 2026-11-16): iOS-only Connect, Tolerance, Snap-to-Linear controls (Windows has no UI for those columns): Not a defect: the columns are part of the OST schema and Windows round-trips them untouched (its update writes only changed fields), it just has no UI for them.
- **GEO-15** (decided 2026-11-16): area meshes: Windows inward (negative signed volume), iOS outward: Not a separate defect: inward in-memory winding of the area, linear and count generators is the Windows convention (the OBJ writer's face swap and the Python flip after the boolean are built around it).
- **MSH-21** (decided 2026-11-16): flat quantized face shading on iOS instead of Windows' 60-degree crease-angle smooth shading: Not a Windows defect: crease-angle smooth shading is the intended Windows look; flat shading on iOS is a deliberate rendering-layer choice (PORTING_LOG 2026-03-23).
- **RPV-22** (decided 2026-11-16): curved linear takeoffs: no curved placement and no centre handle on iOS: Not a Windows defect: Windows supports curved linears (arc band, three handles, 6-value position); iOS scope decision only.
- **CAM-02** (decided 2026-11-16): camera refit on every empty-to-non-empty page selection on iOS; Windows keeps the camera within a bid: Not a Windows defect: the camera is fitted once per bid scene and kept across same-bid scene updates (pinned); the iOS transition refit is a deliberate iOS addition.
- **COND-PLACE-01** (not decided, still open): note 1: placement left running when the Image layer is hidden: Only an edge case: a condition assigned to the Image layer is hidden and unplaceable after the Image layer is hidden, while the placement tool stays armed (neither suspended nor restored).
- **DLG-FINITE-01** (decided 2026-11-19): whitespace-only Rise / Run: Windows rejects, old iOS accepted as 0: Not a defect: Windows rejects whitespace-only Rise and Run with 'Rise must be a number.' / 'Run must be a number.' (an empty field means 0).
- **UND-09** (decided 2026-11-19): 'Count as Negative Quantity' acts on the plan-view selected takeoffs only: Not a defect: Windows changes only the takeoffs passed in (the plan-view selection) and pushes one local history entry; this is the behaviour the iOS code change copies.

### Still open

This section was called "Needs my decision" until 2026-11-19 (the anchor `#needs-my-decision` still works): its 8 formal items and its 15 candidate items were decided that day (see above). What the user has NOT decided stays here, with the Windows file:line where one exists, and nothing below changes any behaviour until it is decided. Machine-readable: `header.needsMyDecision.open` in `PARITY_DIVERGENCES.json`. **20 open entries** = 3 questions + 10 FYI notes + 7 iOS-internal cross-cluster issues; 3 further cross-cluster entries are listed for completeness (1 stale and resolved, 2 informational).

#### Questions: 3 (nothing decided yet)

- **DLG-FINITE-01** (condition number above the int range): Condition number above the int range. WINDOWS edit_condition_dialog.py:1449-1469: int(text) with no upper bound, only a value below 1 is refused, so any whole number the column holds is accepted (up to 64 bits). IOS: ConditionFormRules.cpp parse_condition_number refuses 2147483648 and up (Condition::ref_no is an int, INT_MAX). ASK: widen Condition::ref_no and the column reads to 64 bits, or keep refusing 2147483648 and up? (The whitespace-only Rise/Run half was decided on 2026-11-19 and is done.) Windows file:line: ost_visualizer/presentation/dialogs/edit_condition_dialog.py:1449-1469.
- **COND-PLACE-01** (note 1: Image-layer exemption on hide): Hiding the Image layer. WINDOWS ui_event_coordinator.py:6473-6476 skips _suspend_active_layer_tool when the hidden layer is the Image layer (and :6503-6504 skips the restore), so a placement of a condition on the Image layer keeps running on a condition that is hidden and unplaceable (a tap is ignored, placement_mode.py:1648). IOS: _suspendPlacementForLayerHide treats the Image layer like any other layer and leaves the placement tool. ASK: copy the exemption? (judged an edge case, not a Windows defect; low confidence) Windows file:line: ost_visualizer/presentation/coordinators/ui_event_coordinator.py:6473-6476; ost_visualizer/presentation/coordinators/ui_event_coordinator.py:6503-6504; ost_visualizer/presentation/coordinators/placement_coordinator.py:127-129; ost_visualizer/presentation/coordinators/placement_coordinator.py:163-189; ost_visualizer/domain/services/project_data_service.py:651-653; ost_visualizer/presentation/components/plan_view/components/placement_mode.py:1648.
- **COND-PLACE-01** (note 2: layer-less conditions on Hide All): Hide All with an armed placement of a layer-less condition. WINDOWS ui_event_coordinator.py:1473-1489 never suspends the tool for a layer-less condition (pinned by a test) but project_data_service.py:664-678 marks every condition hidden on Hide All, so the tool stays armed on an unplaceable condition (possible Windows defect, consequence of LP-22). IOS: a layer-less condition is never suspended either (suspended_placement_for_layer_hide); Hide All writes only layer visibility and a layer-less condition stays visible and placeable (LP-22 stays wont-fix). ASK: nothing to port unless the Windows behavior is wanted byte for byte. Windows file:line: ost_visualizer/presentation/coordinators/ui_event_coordinator.py:1473-1489; ost_visualizer/presentation/coordinators/ui_event_coordinator.py:6721-6724; ost_visualizer/domain/services/project_data_service.py:664-678; ost_visualizer/presentation/coordinators/placement_coordinator.py:127-129; ost_visualizer/presentation/components/plan_view/components/placement_mode.py:1647-1650; tests/presentation/coordinators/test_ui_event_coordinator.py:18860-18864. possible Windows defect: ost_visualizer/domain/services/project_data_service.py:664-678: Hide All hides layer-less conditions in memory only.

#### FYI: 10 notes, no decision needed

- IMP-14: an unreadable / non-cabinet package says "Invalid OSP file format" where Windows says "found 0" (kept iOS wording of wiring entry IMP-M03).
- DBP-11: iOS failure dialogs keep the Windows hint "The database may be locked by another application."; "New Project / Failed to create project.", "Save Failed / Failed to save the display settings.", "Duplicate Error", "Delete Error" and the "Not Saved" / "Layer Visibility" alerts for failed forward writes are iOS wording where Windows only logs.
- GEO-M02: Qt clipper tolerance band cannot be reproduced; iOS uses one tolerance (1e-12 of the candidate size + 8 ulp); identical to Windows for exactly touching geometry and beyond ~1e-7 relative.
- Import: iOS is deliberately STRICTER than the Windows cabinet library on inconsistent cabinet headers.
- Quantities: Windows math.ceil raising on a non-finite quotient is not reproduced (no throwing from the quantity path); parsers: feet digit runs of 309+ digits (Windows OverflowError) return (0.0,false) on iOS; formatting: Windows quirks ported as is (format_quantity_number prints -0 / -0.00, display_to_mm strips any run of trailing m).
- Export rules: a cut positive whose faces were all removed by remove_degenerate_faces is not counted as surviving (Windows would export an empty file); meshing: the Windows ERROR log of a failed boolean_union is not ported (only the WARNING).
- Plan view: a stored negative or non-finite BidPages Width/Height is treated as missing (612 x 792) where Windows carries it into the transform; a rotation that is not a multiple of 90 draws an unrotated canvas and no takeoffs; image layers named image are decided by smallest sequence then uid.
- Plan view: Windows draws small mid handles on the in-progress area polyline preview; iOS only on the rectangle being dragged (RPV-32 text).
- Services: LP-12 write-back of the saved page on pick is not ported (optional per item); load_project fails a bid with two BidSettings rows like Windows.
- ViewController: undo of a delete selects only the deleted takeoff (iOS has one selected takeoff); pre-existing hazard: a takeoff selected in 3D stays selected after switching to 2D although its page may not be the 2D page.

#### Cross-cluster issues (iOS-internal, no Windows behaviour involved): 7 open

- **XCI-02**: Duplicate local logic: four near-identical "present an OK alert from the top-most presented controller" helpers in ViewController categories written by different clusters: ViewController+Conditions.mm _presentConditionAlertWithTitle:message: (U4), ViewController.mm _presentSaveFailure (U3), ViewController+Layers.mm _presentLayerVisibilityFailure (U3), ViewController+Export.mm _presentExportAlertWithTitle:message: (U1, presents on self without walking presentedViewController). _presentSaveFailure and _presentLayerVisibilityFailure could call the existing generic helper. Cosmetic, no behaviour difference except the export alert.
- **XCI-03**: Duplicate predicate: PlanView.mm isCountLike() (U1, RPV-17; single use at :527) repeats the count-or-attachment test that Core/Geometry/takeoff_footprint.cpp:292 already makes. Harmless.
- **XCI-04**: Leftovers after the wiring (no caller, kept by tests): ost_store::shift_condition_ref_nos (the update now shifts atomically), ImportService +loadProjectWithUID: (one-argument convenience, no caller), ost_meshing::generate_takeoff_meshes (thin wrapper, tests only), RenderConfig::is_ost() deprecated alias (two tests). No duplicate implementation of the same rule was found.
- **XCI-05**: Double work on open (performance, not logic): DatabaseViewController preflights the bid with loadProjectWithUID:project:error: and ViewController _loadProjectFromDatabase: loads it again.
- **XCI-06**: Hole grouping runs over all takeoffs of the page (like the old hole map), not only visible ones (U1 RPV-17 / U2b RPV-31 share Core group_area_takeoffs_with_holes; no second implementation).
- **XCI-07**: Edit-pan start: PlanView gestureRecognizerShouldBegin: for the edit pan hit-tests at the recognition location (after the pan threshold), not at touch-down (U2a note); RPV-M07 only fixes the start point. PlanView clearTapSelection also clears _draggingUID (a page-bar tap by a second finger during a drag would drop didEndDrag).
- **XCI-08**: Tooling: check_objc_selftest.ps1 case "a" fails with "anchor text not found in Services/ExportService.mm" because U1 rewrote that file (the anchor text of the selftest, not the checker or the code); 5 of 6 selftest cases pass and the "repair" case reports all 4 baseline call sites already wired. (This concerns the checker used here, which is kept outside the repo; no repo file is affected.)

Also listed for completeness:

- **XCI-01** (stale, resolved): OPEN HAND-OFF (U4 -> U3, COND-PLACE-01): Controllers/ViewController/Components/ViewController+Layers.mm still does not reload the Conditions list after a layer is shown/hidden (greyed rows are computed when the list loads) and does not suspend / restore an active placement when its layer is hidden / shown (Windows does). Nobody executed it; item stays partial. Resolved by COND-PLACE-01 (status fixed-ui-unverified): ViewController+Layers.mm now has _suspendPlacementForLayerHide / _restorePlacementAfterLayerShow and _refreshConditionsListForLayerVisibility; the two COND-PLACE-01 notes above are what remains of that item.
- **XCI-09** (informational, nothing to do): Checked and clean: callers of every changed signature (canvas_delta_to_ost, ost_to_pdf_coordinates, loadPage, handleDrag, select2DPage, export*, updateCondition*, undo delegate methods) are updated (27/27 platform files compile, 4 baseline protocol warnings); no file was edited by two clusters in conflicting ways (ViewController.mm drag region U2a vs undo U3: the UND-07 hand-over uses the BOOL of undoRedoApplyPositions:; PlanView.mm U1/U2a/U2b: one hole grouping, one gray/alpha path; Layer.hpp normalize_layer_name U2b/U3: duplicate removed); no Core function was implemented twice by different clusters.
- **XCI-10** (informational, nothing to do): Stub additions (all real Apple APIs with their real signatures): U2b CoreGraphics.h CGContextEOFillPath, CGContextEOClip, CGContextGetClipBoundingBox, CGPathAddRect, CGPDFPageGetBoxRect, CGPDFPageGetRotationAngle; U4 UIControl.h UITextSmartQuotesType / UITextSmartDashesType and UITextField smartQuotesType / smartDashesType (iOS 11). No other stub was added.

## (F) Cannot reproduce on iOS

- **EXP-24** (ui-cosmetic): DXF LINE entity order is the std::unordered_set iteration order of the STL in use. Evidence: Windows `cpp_extensions/src/mesh/feature_edges.cpp:109-175; exporters/dxf_exporter.py:32-38`; iOS `Core/Geometry/feature_edges.cpp:109-173` (the `std::unordered_set` is at :124) and `Services/ExportService.mm:151`. CAD semantics are order-independent. The DXFWriter itself is byte-identical for the same line list. Verifier: LINE entity order follows unordered_set iteration (feature_edges.cpp:109-175), which is MSVC STL specific and cannot be matched under libc++. ... Final status: cannot-reproduce (nothing to port).
- Partial: exact Qt/locale ordering of folder names (BRW-M01) and the machine-dependent ANSI code page of legacy CAB member names (IMP-18) cannot be reproduced bit for bit. Both were decisions in section E and are fixed with the closest reproducible rule (BRW-M01: case-insensitive ASCII compare, lowercase first, then UID; IMP-18: cp1252 for names without the UTF-8 flag).

## (H) Windows features with no iOS counterpart (list only)

From the Windows-only inventory (87 features). `[platform-only]` = tied to the Windows platform (not a candidate for iOS). This is the Phase 1 inventory. The rule for the fix phase was core logic equal to Windows, only the platform layer adapted, and NO new features, so none of it was scheduled. A few entries turned out to be parity items that sections A-D already counted and are implemented there; they are marked `[implemented ...]` below. All other entries are unchanged.

### ai-takeoff

- AI takeoff analysis (PDF linework, text and region finding): Read-side AI tools: sheet listing with scale hints, PDF text and vector segment extraction, closed-region finding with door-gap closing and dashed-outline handling, quant ...
- AI takeoff proposals (scale and slab elements): propose_scale, propose_element and polygon simplification that build slab conditions and takeoffs from detected regions, with high-impact assumption tracking (scale, thic ...
- AI changeset review, apply, undo and discard: Review dialog showing outlines, added area/volume and assumptions (Accept disabled until high-impact ones are settled). ...
- AI assumptions sidecar and audit log: Per-bid assumptions saved under ~/.ost_visualizer/ai_takeoff, a rebind prompt when the database moves, and an audit log of tool calls and decisions.
- AI takeoff MCP helper, local pipe bridge and MCP Setup tab [platform-only]: ostv-takeoff-mcp stdio helper exposing 15 tools, talking to the running app over a user-only named pipe with a rotating session key. ...
- Read-only MCP server for databases (ost-visualizer) [platform-only]: ostv-mcp stdio helper exposing about 40 bounded read tools and prompts (projects, bids, pages, conditions, takeoffs, summary, PDF text/vector summaries, scope-gap checks) ...

### annotation

- Annotation drawing tools and rendering: Place, select, resize, rotate and render line, arrow, dimension, rectangle, oval, polygon, cloud, ink, highlight and text annotations (plus callout, hotlink and named-vie ...
- Annotation persistence, text lifecycle and history: Insert, delete and save annotation positions, styles and text properties (Latin-1 BidTexts/BidCallOuts encoding rules, page-scale rescaling) with annotation-aware undo/re ...
- Annotation styles, fonts and creation defaults: Per-annotation color, line width, font name/size/bold/italic/underline and text alignment, with normalization rules. ...
- Hotlinks: Hotlink markers on a page that jump to a named view or page. Config.hotlink_target chooses whether the target opens in the Annotation window, the View window or Main.
- Named views: Named rectangular regions on a page (create via tool, pick via Select Named View dialog, rect-to-position normalization) used as navigation targets for hotlinks and detac ...
- Annotation window and View window (detached plan windows): Detached secondary plan windows showing a page or named view, kept in sync with Main (takeoffs, layers, annotations, remote changes). Also a detached 3D Mesh window. ...
- Takeoff condition labels on the 2D plan: Display Name and Display Dimension labels on 2D takeoffs, with area label and style label text, draggable and editable (font, color, position) and persisted. ...

### business

- Bid comparison by condition ref number: Compares an old/source bid with a new/target bid by condition RefNo and returns bounded aggregates by condition type with affected pages. ...
- Cover Sheet (bid properties): Edit bid name, number, job ID, bid date, notes, estimator, job status, measure base, takeoff increments, scale style and new-page defaults, and open Areas, Employees and ...
- Summary tab (condition quantity summary): Review quantities grouped by Area, Type or Page with unused conditions hidden, multi-area totals, and copy/delete rows. ...
- Project tree structure editing (new bid, duplicate, rename, copy/cut/paste bids): Create a new project or bid, duplicate a bid, rename bids and projects, copy/cut/paste bids across projects, and Restore. ...
- Job status grouping, change and bid lock: Group projects by job status, change a bid's status, and block editing when the status is marked Locked. iOS has no lock guard.
- Employees master data: Master > Employees dialog with list and detail editing (name, address, phones, email, pay class), also used as estimator picker.
- Payroll classes master data: Master > Payroll Classes editor for pay classes.
- Job statuses master data: Master > Job Statuses editor (ordering, locked flag, in-use protection).
- Condition types master data: Master > Condition Types editor (CdnTypes) with in-use delete blocking and picker mode. iOS can only pick an existing type.
- Bid areas (Areas dialog, area combo, page area): Hierarchical bid areas (new, delete, indent, outdent, move) selectable per page and used in Summary grouping. iOS never surfaces BidAreas.
- Metric units, dimension display and quantity formatting: Metric UOMs (mm, mm2, m, m2, mm3, m3), metric/imperial normalization, metric dimension and quantity display, and metric takeoff settings. ... [implemented by the fix phase as COND-METRIC-01, QTY-05, QTY-06, QTY-12 (fixed)]
- Quantity rounding and attachment quantity aggregation: Applies round_quantity/round_up to calculated totals and aggregates attachment footprint and perimeter into parent area quantities. ... [implemented by the fix phase as QTY-04, QTY-02 (fixed)]

### collaboration

- Microsoft SQL Server database backend: Find, connect to, create and initialize SQL Server databases (Windows or SQL auth, TLS options, schema-v1 validation and provisioning), browsed alongside other databases. ...
- Multi-user SQL collaboration engine: Per-database sessions with heartbeat and bid presence, expiring edit leases, optimistic row versions, Change Tracking feed with remote reconciliation, FIFO write queue, l ...
- SQL Server credentials in Windows Credential Manager and Windows authentication [platform-only]: SQL passwords stored in Windows Credential Manager, Windows-integrated authentication, and ODBC Driver 18 dependency.

### editing

- Cut, copy, paste and duplicate takeoffs and annotations: Plan-item clipboard across pages and bids (reference remapping, parent/hole relinking, one transaction), Ctrl+C/X/V/D, Intelligent Paste axis snap, and conditions carried ...
- Paste Backout: Paste copied backout (hole) takeoffs into a target parent area with a live preview and validity checks.
- Backout mode (place holes inside an area): Tools > Backout Mode: choose a parent area and draw backout areas inside it with containment validation. ...
- Rotate and flip selected takeoffs (single and group): Rotate Takeoff Left/Right, Flip Horizontal/Vertical and a rotate handle, with group rotation that moves area children, saved and undoable.
- Slope direction rotate handle: On-plan handle to set the slope direction of sloped area and linear takeoffs. iOS meshes slopes but has no plan handle.
- Set as Curved Segment and polygon control points: Context-menu toggle to curve a linear segment, and Add or Subtract Control Point on polygons and lines.
- Assign to Current Area and Select Objects in Current Area: Assign selected takeoffs to the page's current bid area, and select all objects belonging to the current area.
- Reassign takeoffs to another condition: Context-menu submenu that moves selected takeoffs to a different condition, with impact checks.
- Marquee (roping) selection and Select All: Rubber-band selection with touching or inclusive method (Options), Ctrl+A, and multi-item selection and move. ...
- Snap to PDF lines, takeoffs and right angles: Snap index for PDF line ends, midpoints, crossings and edges, existing takeoff geometry, and right angles, with per-snap enable and pixel thresholds plus separate pressed ...
- Cursor and pointer preferences [platform-only]: Full-window crosshairs with color and thickness, auto zoom level, and advanced mouse controls.
- Zoom tool, zoom percentage selector and view commands: Zoom-in/out/reset commands, zoom percentage combo (separate drafts for plan and 3D), previous/next page commands and a Zoom cursor tool.
- PDF text selection and copy: Select and copy text runs from the page PDF on the plan, with a PDF-text context menu.
- Complete undo/redo history: Edit > Undo/Redo covers takeoff add, delete, move, rotate and property changes, annotations, condition and folder edits, layer and area changes, group rotation, AI change ...
- Create conditions and condition folders: New condition, new condition folder, rename, delete and drag-drop into folders, group by condition type, placeable-state display, and impact checks. ...
- Condition copy, cut and paste (across bids): Copy and cut conditions in the Conditions sidebar and paste them into another bid or folder.
- Duplicate and reassign takeoff; Select Objects by condition: Right-click a condition to duplicate it and move only the displayed page's takeoffs to the copy in one transaction, or to select that condition's takeoffs on the page.
- Renumber Conditions: Project > Renumber Conditions rewrites condition reference numbers for a bid. iOS only shifts RefNos on duplicate.
- Layer management and default layers: Create, rename, delete (blocked when in use), reorder, check/uncheck all and edit default layers, including special Annotations, Comments and Image layers whose visibilit ...
- Edit actions from the 3D view context menu: Delete, Assign to Area, Reassign Condition, Count as Negative and Set Curved from the 3D view. iOS 3D long-press offers only Count as Negative Quantity.

### export

- OST export (.ost): Export the selected bid back to an OST file (raw-table serialization with Latin-1 rules). Needed for round trip to desktop. iOS has no writer.
- OSP export (.osp package): Package the bid plus its page images into a CAB-based OSP with safe member paths. iOS can only extract CAB files.
- PDF export with takeoff and annotation markup: Per-page PDF export drawing takeoffs and all annotation types as vector markup over the page, overlay or composite background (or blank), using PDFium and QPDF.
- PDF annotation captions (Bluebeam-compatible): Options > Export lets users choose which measurement captions appear on exported takeoff annotations in the PDF.
- FBX export: Mesh export to FBX through the shared exporter base.
- Summary CSV export: Export the Summary tab (grouped by Area, Type or Page) to CSV with natural/type/page/area sorting and optional condition-name elevation dropping.
- Full-featured HTML 3D export viewer: The Windows HTML export has a 2D plan tab with the PDF and SVG takeoff overlay, page combo, Visibility panel (by Layer, Condition or Area), hover tip with elevations/SF/C ...
- Elevation callouts in HTML and PDF exports: Visibility-aware callouts with condition name, top and bottom elevation (nearest 1/8 in) and cubic yards, each independently toggled, with color options for HTML and PDF.
- Drop condition elevation from names in OST/OSP and CSV exports: Export options that strip the elevation suffix from condition names.

### other

- Multi-database registry (Open Databases dialog): Register several databases (Access and SQL), check or uncheck them to load or unload, remove entries, and persist the list (file_state.json). ...
- Pan sidebar (mini-map): Docked thumbnail of the current page showing the visible area; click or drag to pan Main's plan view.
- Tower crane visualization ([crane] notes tag): Count conditions whose notes contain a [crane model=... mast= jib= counterjib= trolley= hook= hookheight=] tag show a GLB tower crane model (classic, liebherr) on the foo ...
- 3D section cuts (north-south and east-west): Two sliders clip the model with capped cut faces, outlines and pickable caps; labels in feet from the model edge.
- 3D feature-edge overlay toggle: Options and 3D checkbox to draw feature edges for the whole scene (boundary edges and creases over 15 degrees). iOS draws edges only as a selection highlight.
- Saved per-bid 3D camera state: Remembers and restores the 3D camera per bid across scene refreshes and remote updates.
- Separate 2D and 3D takeoff display modes with sync toggle: View > Takeoff: Transparent, Solid or Original for 2D and 3D independently, a Sync 2D/3D toggle, and grayscale. iOS has one color mode for both views.
- Count condition shape meshes (ellipse, triangle, rhombus, hexagon, octagon): Elliptical cylinder with width/depth and triangle, rhombus, hexagon and octagon prisms. iOS builds only box and circular cylinder. [implemented by the fix phase as MSH-08, MSH-09, MSH-10, MSH-11 (fixed)]

### page-settings

- Set Scale dialog and page settings bar: Edit page scale (Architectural, Civil, Metric or custom), apply to all pages, and page-bar scale and area combos with rescaling of existing positions.
- Rename page and delete page: Edit > Rename Page and Delete Page, with deletion cascading to the page's takeoffs and annotations.
- Plan Organizer (add, duplicate, import and move pages and page folders): Cover Sheet tree to add pages and folders, duplicate pages, import PDF/TIFF files as pages (multi-page rows), drag to reorder and set per-page image, size, scale and show ...
- Adjust Images dialog and Invert/Bitonal toggles: Image menu and Ctrl+I: rotation (0/90/180/270), flip X/Y, invert and bitonal rendering of the page image, optionally for all pages, persisted per page. ...
- Rotate and flip page image commands: Rotate Image Left/Right and Flip Image Horizontal/Vertical, separate from rotating or flipping takeoffs.
- Overlay image (select, remove, move, resize, show): Attach a second image/PDF to a page, move and resize it via the overlay rect, remove it, and composite it on the plan. iOS loads the overlay fields but never draws them.
- Page show mode (Original, Overlay, Both): Show Original Image and Show Overlay Image toggles in the Image and context menus, persisted per page.
- Multi-page PDF page index and TIFF raster pages: Select which page of a multi-page PDF a sheet uses, and support TIF/TIFF raster page images (including in OSP). ... [the PDF page index half is implemented by the fix phase as LP-16 (fixed-ui-unverified)]
- Page image plane in the 3D view: Page image, overlay or composite is shown as a textured plane under the 3D model with the page's mode, rotation and effects.
- Persisted page view state and selected page: Saves each page's zoom and view center and each bid's selected page in the database with debounced deferred persistence.
- Missing page file and render-progress notices: Overlay bar when a page image or PDF cannot be found or loaded, plus a page render loading bar and PDF render scale and prefetch logic.
- Page name display options (page index and sheet number): Options to show the PDF page index and the sheet number alongside sheet names in page lists.

- Windows installer, file associations and build tooling [platform-only]: MSI installer, .ost/.osp registry associations, Nuitka/CMake build scripts and bundled MCP helper executables.
- Desktop menu bar, toolbars, sidebars, status bar and keyboard shortcuts [platform-only]: File/Edit/View/Tools/Image/Project/Master/Help menus, toggleable toolbars and docked sidebars, status bar with date, license and collaboration state, Ctrl-key shortcuts, ...
- Takeoff toolbar customization [platform-only]: Options > Takeoff Toolbar to hide or show individual navigation, cursor, annotation, zoom and Page Settings controls, with Restore Default Toolbar.
- Options dialog and persisted app configuration [platform-only]: Options tabs (Options, Takeoff Toolbar, Fonts & Colors, Export, MCP Setup), Apply/OK/Cancel, reset all settings, and persisted config.json and workspace state (window geo ...
- Windows light/dark theme following [platform-only]: Qt Fusion or Windows 11 style, palette and title-bar tracking of the Windows color scheme across Main and detached windows.

- (project-browser) Duplicate bid; copy, cut and paste bids (including paste into another folder)
- (project-browser) Drag-and-drop move of bids between folders
- (project-browser) Empty Deleted Bids Folder (permanently delete all trashed bids)
- (project-browser) Multi-select delete/trash/restore across several bids or folders, with mixed permanent-plus-trash handling
- (project-browser) Import into a chosen target folder (selected tree node), with the 'imported as orphaned because Deleted Bids cannot be a target' rule and an import result summary
- (project-browser) Multi-database project tree (open, close, new database; file roots)
- (project-browser) Group by Job Status, Change Job Status, and bid locking via job status (blocks trashing/moving the active locked bid)
- (project-browser) Bid columns (No., Name, Status, Bid Date, Job No., Estimator, Pages, Conditions, Notes, Copy From, Copy Timestamp) and column sorting
- (project-browser) Renumber Conditions from the project tree
- (project-browser) Condition folder insert/rename/delete and nested condition-folder hierarchy (BidConditionFolder.parent_uid, cycle check)
- (project-browser) Remembering the selected page per bid and the project-tree workspace state (selection, expanded nodes)
- (project-browser) Per-bid read of page and condition counts shown in the tree, and background bid load/prepare/apply with stale-version checks
- (settings-undo-camera) Separate 2D and 3D display modes plus the 'Sync 2D/3D display modes' flag (default synced; the 3D mode wins when sync is turned on)
- (settings-undo-camera) Global Edges toggle (show_edges) and tower crane toggle (show_tower_crane, default on) for 3D and HTML
- (settings-undo-camera) Options dialog and validated JSON config: fonts, colours, crosshair, toolbar prefs, elevation callouts, captions, hotlink and roping settings, inactive_object_color, auto-zoom level, AI takeoff flag
- (settings-undo-camera) Configurable snap settings: mouse snap angles (valid set 0,1,2,3,4,5,10,15,30,45,90), Shift modifier, snap-to-grid, PDF-line, takeoff and right-angle thresholds (default 8 px), snap index
- (settings-undo-camera) Keyboard arrow nudge of selected takeoffs by one snap increment
- (settings-undo-camera) Per-bid saved and restored 3D camera state (cache with eviction)
- (settings-undo-camera) Camera inertia, damping and velocity clamps; rotate and pan velocities
- (settings-undo-camera) Section cuts (north-south, east-west sliders with feet read-out)
- (settings-undo-camera) Zoom-percent combo and scene navigation controls; set_zoom_percent and wheel zoom
- (settings-undo-camera) 3D page-plane (plan texture) included in fit bounds
- (settings-undo-camera) Persist and restore the last selected page per bid (BidSettings.BidPageSelectedUID)
- (settings-undo-camera) Global Settings defaults and BidSettings reading (ScaleStyle, ScaleFactor1/2, PageWidth/Height, MeasureBase, TakeoffIncrements, NextBidNo)
- (settings-undo-camera) Undo history for rotate, paste, property edits (condition, area, curve), annotations, AI changesets; labelled entries; discard_entry; lifetime targets, suspension and rebind on restore; ...
- (settings-undo-camera) Inactive-area object colour (area selection)
- (settings-undo-camera) Desktop viewer cursors (zoom, rotate, move overlay)

## (I) Stale claims in the existing iOS docs

HISTORICAL record (Phase 1, 2026-11-15) of what the OLD porting docs claimed. The docs were PORTING_STATUS.md, PORTING_MAP.md, PORTING_PLAN.md, PORTING_BLOCKERS.md, PORTING_STATUS.json and PORTING_LOG.md (repo root; there is no docs folder). They have been rewritten since (commit 42b46c3 and the final documentation pass), so do not read this table as a description of the current docs or of the current code: the `Reality` column describes the code as mapped, before the fixes, and the outcome of each referenced item is in sections A-D. Claims reported by several areas are listed once.

| Doc | Claim | Reality (evidence) | Reported by |
|---|---|---|---|
| PORTING_STATUS.md:40 (also PORTING_STATUS.json 'Measurement and quantity calculations', PORTING_BLOCKERS.md:65 ... | Quantity calculations: 'Imperial formulas, holes, slope, curved length, grid, and negative aggregation exist in UomService.hpp.' Missing items are listed as metric UOMs, normalization, attachment aggr ... | Flat formulas, grid, holes and negative sums were verified to match Windows (5000 random cases, 0 diffs). 'Slope' is wrong for areas with negative rise/run (QTY-10). 'Curved length' is wrong in three ways (QTY-07/08/09). Hole aggregation is wrong for non-area children and foreign pages (QTY-03). Attachments with a parent are skipped entirely (QTY-01), a gap the docs do not mention. | quantities |
| PORTING_BLOCKERS.md:65 and PORTING_LOG.md:71 | Attachment quantity parity: 'iOS quantity formulas accept attachment footprint/perimeter, but compute_page_quantities passes TODO zero values.' | True for footprint/perimeter (UomService.hpp:790-791), but incomplete. compute_page_quantities also drops Attachment/non-area takeoffs that have a parent_uid (UomService.hpp:767), so an attachment's own count is 0 (QTY-01). | quantities |
| PORTING_BLOCKERS.md:64 and PORTING_MAP.md:62 | Metric UOM support missing: 'iOS UomService.hpp defines only imperial codes 0-9' and 'metric/imperial normalization are missing'. | Accurate but understated. Codes 11-16 silently convert with divisor 1.0 (raw inches shown with an empty label), the picker is not metric-aware, and labels 4/7 are wrong ('SQ IN'/'ROOF' vs 'IN²'/'SQ') with roofing missing from the area picker. Bid.measure_base is loaded but unused (QTY-05, QTY-06, QTY-16). | quantities |
| PORTING_MAP.md:63 | condition_quantity_service.py -> UomService.hpp / ConditionsViewController.mm: 'Page condition totals are displayed ... formula parity needs fixtures.' | Parity now checked by differential test: formulas match, aggregation and page-scope do not. Sidebar totals use the 2D page + 3D pages union regardless of mode and are not refreshed on 2D page change (QTY-11, QTY-15). Displayed numbers lack metric decimals and zero text (QTY-12, QTY-22). | quantities |
| PORTING_PLAN.md:46 and PORTING_STATUS.md:61 | Open task: 'Verify rounding, grid counts, holes, negative quantities, slope, and curved length calculations against desktop.' | Done in this pass. Grid counts, negative quantities and flat holes match. Rounding is not applied (QTY-04), area slope (QTY-10) and curved length (QTY-07/08/09) differ, and hole/attachment aggregation differs (QTY-01/02/03). | quantities |
| PORTING_BLOCKERS.md:26-27 (B-001) and PORTING_STATUS.json B-001 'needs_verification' | B-001 'Replaced': curved/sloped geometry parity needs fixture comparison only. | The iOS arc code predates Windows' arc-side fix (owner decision 2026-11-06, cited in test_uom_service.py UomArcControlPointSideTests docstring) and the straight-chord rule. Core/Domain/UomService.hpp and Core/Meshing/gen_curved.cpp:81-95 both still use the legacy cross-product direction rule. | quantities |
| PORTING_MAP.md:65 and PORTING_BLOCKERS.md:70 | 'Source-confirmed application of round_quantity/round_up in calculated totals was not found' and the plan item 'Apply or explicitly defer round_quantity / round_up'. | Confirmed (never applied), but it is not deferrable under the parity rule: Windows applies ceil(value/increment)*increment per takeoff before the sign (QTY-04). | quantities |
| PORTING_STATUS.md:45, PORTING_MAP.md:106, PORTING_BLOCKERS.md:72 | 'No iOS app test target, fixture suite ... was found.' | An untracked Tests/ host harness (CMakeLists.txt, Support/TestHarness.hpp, README.md) appeared in the iOS working tree during this pass (created by another step, not by this mapper). It has no quantity tests yet. UomService.hpp compiles standalone on MSVC and was the basis for the differential harness. | quantities |
| PORTING_MAP.md:50 (Geometry, Meshing, And Booleans table) | `cpp_extensions/src/common`, `mesh`, `geom_utils`, `coord_transform`, `earcut` -> `Core/Geometry/*`: Ported. Portable C++ geometry modules are present. | Only earcut, vec_types and mesh_data match Windows. geom_utils lacks point_to_segment_distance and its polygon_is_valid/signed_area are the old weaker versions (GEO-07, GEO-09). coord_transform.cpp is a stale API: Windows now has the 8-argument validated ost_to_pdf_coordinates plus transform_*_to_2d/3d and common/page_transform.hpp; ... | geometry |
| PORTING_LOG.md:192-218 (Phase 1: Portable C++ Core Copied) | geom_utils.cpp and coord_transform.cpp were copied as 'direct copy' from the desktop C++ extensions. | The Windows geom_utils (polygon_is_valid, signed_area, point_to_segment_distance) and coord_transform (ost_to_pdf_coordinates, page_transform.hpp) have since changed substantially; the iOS copies are a snapshot of an older revision. | geometry |
| PORTING_LOG.md:481-487 (Phase 4 coordinate transformation approach) | iOS passes sr=1/(sf2/sf1) to MeshFactory 'This matches the desktop pipeline: transform_to_3d(ost_x, ost_y, scale_ratio) = {-(ost_x/ratio), ost_y/ratio}'. | The current Windows 3D pipeline builds its mesh generator with a default scale-1 coordinate system (visualization_provider.py:114), so 3D geometry is raw OST units and the page ratio is never applied; test_page_flip_mesh.py:97-99 pins X in [-10,-4] for ratio 4 (GEO-01). The ratio-dividing transform_to_3d is only used by the per-page rotation/flip box, with ratio 1. | geometry |
| PORTING_LOG.md:1492-1497 (Phase 9: Area slope) | Per-vertex Y ... 'Ascending/descending logic matches desktop calc_area_mesh_verts'. | Windows area slope always rises along the rotated projection and ignores the sign of rise/run (test_area.py::test_area_mesh_slope_direction_uses_rotation_not_rise_run_signs); iOS flips descending slopes and uses the opposite rotation sense in its mirrored frame (GEO-05). | geometry |
| PORTING_LOG.md:1523-1526 (Phase 9: Hole grouping) | `build_hole_map` scans takeoffs for children (is_hole()==true) and groups by parent_uid, matching the desktop approach. | Windows group_area_takeoffs_with_holes only treats area-condition children whose parent is an area takeoff as holes; attachments, orphan holes and holes under non-area parents stay as normal takeoffs (GEO-04). The export copy also has a sign bug (GEO-02). | geometry |
| PORTING_LOG.md:1220-1226 (Phase 7: X coordinate negation 'corrects the mirroring difference') | X negation in MeshFactory (-x, with y) corrects the mirroring difference to desktop. | Partially superseded: iOS now also negates Y into Z (a Y-up proper-rotation adaptation, fine), but page rotation and flip transforms were never ported and Windows applies a (-x, +y) frame with face reversal for single flips (GEO-03). | geometry |
| PORTING_STATUS.md:22 and PORTING_MAP.md:51-55 (Mesh generation / geometry: area with holes and slope 'source-p ... | Area with holes, slope and curved meshes are source-present and need only fixture comparison. | Concrete numeric mismatches with Windows were found for area meshes: scale (GEO-01), rotation/flip (GEO-03), holes in export (GEO-02, GEO-04), slope direction (GEO-05), tessellation (GEO-14) and winding (GEO-15). A Windows-derived unit test suite would catch each of them. | geometry |
| PORTING_LOG.md (2026-03-23 Phase 4, 'Coordinate transformation approach') | iOS computes scale_ratio = sf2/sf1 and passes sr = 1/scale_ratio; 'This matches the desktop pipeline: transform_to_3d(ost_x, ost_y, scale_ratio)'. | The Windows mesh pipeline builds its coordinate system with CoordinateTransformerFactory.create() (scale factors 1.0), so meshes are in raw OST units and the page scale is never applied (tests/integration/rendering/test_page_flip_mesh.py::test_mesh_pipeline_preserves_model_scale_when_flipping). See MSH-01. | meshing |
| PORTING_LOG.md (2026-03-23 Phase 9, Area slope) | 'Ascending/descending logic matches desktop calc_area_mesh_verts'. | Windows area slope ignores the signs of rise/run and has its own rotation direction (core/geometry/area.py, pinned by test_area.py). iOS differs in both (MSH-03, MSH-04). | meshing |
| PORTING_LOG.md (2026-03-23 Phase 9, Curved linear mesh) | Arc fit, Bezier fallback and 'adaptive segmentation: 6-64 segments' are ported from the desktop; B-001 resolved. | The curve code is a reimplementation, not a port: segment count rounding, arc side for sweeps above about 180 degrees and the straight-chord rule differ from linear_geom.cpp (MSH-05, MSH-06, MSH-07). | meshing |
| PORTING_LOG.md (2026-03-23 Phase 9, Hole grouping) | build_hole_map groups children (is_hole() == true) by parent_uid into 'scaled/negated hole polygons' for both rendering and export. | Windows only treats area-condition takeoffs whose parent is an area takeoff in the set as holes (group_area_takeoffs_with_holes); and ExportService.mm:33-34 does not negate z for holes, so export holes are mirrored (MSH-12, MSH-14). | meshing |
| PORTING_STATUS.md line 22 (Mesh generation \| Partial) | 'Linear, curved linear, area with holes, count/attachment base meshes, slope, and drop stubs are source-present. Ellipse (width/depth), triangle, rhombus, hexagon and octagon are not partial, they are absent and render as a cylinder or a box. | meshing |
| PORTING_STATUS.md line 23, PORTING_STATUS.json summary.boolean_ops, PORTING_PLAN.md (checked 'Manifold boolean ... | Boolean operations: Ported. | boolean_ops.cpp is identical to Windows, but the orchestration is not: consumed positives stay visible, export applies one rule to all formats, union failure/degenerate handling differs (MSH-15, MSH-16, MSH-19). boolean_ops.cpp:1 also keeps a stale 'TODO: remove guard when Manifold library is linked' comment although no guard exists. | meshing |
| PORTING_MAP.md line 50 (cpp_extensions/src/... coord_transform ... \| Core/Geometry/* \| Ported) | coord_transform and the other C++ geometry modules are portable and ported. | mesh/boolean/earcut/feature_edges/mesh_repair are byte-identical, but Core/Geometry/coord_transform.cpp has an older API (coord_scale, offsets, is_page_rotated, auto_rotate_180) than the Windows ost_to_pdf_coordinates, which now goes through common/page_transform.hpp (validated, quarter turns only). Outside the meshing scope, but the 3D page flip/rotate (MSH-02) depends on it. | meshing |
| PORTING_MAP.md line 54, PORTING_BLOCKERS.md B-001 | linear_geom: curved mesh code exists without GLM; remaining validation is only fixture comparison for curved/sloped cases. | The fixture-style comparison done here shows concrete mismatches for curved walls and sloped areas (MSH-03 to MSH-07). | meshing |
| PORTING_MAP.md line 53, PORTING_BLOCKERS.md 'Count shape mesh parity', PORTING_STATUS.md 'Count condition mesh ... | iOS only routes circle/ellipse to cylinder and all other count shapes to box; desktop has extra paths. | Accurate as written. Shape ids match Windows. | meshing |
